<span style="display:none;" id="data/3PA/plumed_cmap.datcmap">The CONTACTMAP action with label <b>cmap</b> calculates the following quantities:<table  align="center" frame="void" width="95%" cellpadding="5%"><tr><td width="5%"><b> Quantity </b>  </td><td><b> Description </b> </td></tr><tr><td width="5%">cmap.contact</td><td>By not using SUM or CMDIST each contact will be stored in a component</td></tr><tr><td width="5%">cmap.value</td><td>the sum of all the switching function on all the distances</td></tr></table></span></pre></div>
